### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Ammonium carbonate dissociates as
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>COONH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(s)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>2NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow> </math>
In a closed vessel having ammonium carbonate in equilibrium <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is added such that partial pressure of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> now to equals to the original total pressure. The ratio of total pressure now the original pressure is?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>COONH</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>4</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>(s)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mo>&#x21CC;</mo><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>2NH</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>(g)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow></math>
Suppose P is pressure of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> at equilibrium than
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
As <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
On adding <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> at equilibrium
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
So <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
Now total <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
Hence pressure ratio <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>31</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An equilibrium mixture in a vessel of capacity <math> <mrow> <mn>100 litre</mn></mrow> </math> contain <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> </math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo></math> <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> </math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>3</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mol</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>NO</mtext></mrow> </math>. Find number of moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> to be added, so that at new equilibrium the concentration of <math> <mrow> <mtext>NO</mtext></mrow> </math> is <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.04</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mol/lit</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>101</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>101</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>202</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>101</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> …(i)
<math><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></munder><mo>⇌</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></munder></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac></math> …(ii)
Given, <math><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
(i) = (ii)
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>101</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>18</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Acetic acid ( <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> ) is partially dimerised to <math><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> in the vapour phase. At a total pressure of <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.200</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math>, acetic acid is <math> <mrow> <mtext>92</mtext><mtext>.0%</mtext></mrow> </math> dimerized at <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math>. The value of equilibrium constant of dimerization under these conditions is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mn>57.5</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mn>9.7</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mn>97</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <mtext>194</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow>  <mtext>194</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/sszvhsfljabrcittgzklvznoxotxivvvyalcnubrkijnhqkzgx.jpeg" />
According to question; <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.46</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.08</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.2</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.54</mn></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.46</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.2</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.54</mn></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.08</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.2</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.54</mn></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>=194</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>1</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>, initially A was taken. At equilibrium the concentration of D is twice that of A. The value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wtjhsbajtrekzgzvspahfukiqksaiasocetxgewdvupulhcfnb.jpg" />

Now, <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

One of the reaction that takes place in producing steel from iron ore is the reduction of <math> <mrow> <mtext>iron(II)</mtext></mrow> </math> oxide by carbon monoxide to give iron metal and <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>.</mo></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>FeO</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>CO</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext> Fe</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><msub> <mrow> <mtext>; K</mtext></mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.265</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math> at <math> <mrow> <mtext>1050</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math> What are the equilibrium partial pressures of <math> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively at <math> <mrow> <mtext>1050</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> if the initial partial pressures are: <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1.4</mn></math> atm and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.80</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1.739</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.461</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>17.39</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.461</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1.79</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.46</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2.739</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1.461</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1.739</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.461</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/128822.JPG" style="width: 424px; height: 90px;" />

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.265</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1.4</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
On solving, <math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.339</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1.739</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> atm and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.461</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Phosgene,  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> , a poisonous gas decomposes according to the equation

 <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> 

If  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.083</mn></math>  at  <math><mn>900</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> , what is the value of  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.125
- **(B)** 8.0
- **(C)** 6.1
- **(D)** 0.16

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 8.0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup></math> 

where  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>0.082</mn><mtext> L atm mo</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1173</mn></math>  K

So  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.083</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.082</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1173</mn></math> 

 <math><mn>7.98</mn><mo>≃</mo><mn>8.0</mn></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium constant for a reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>⇋</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> is <math><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math> <mrow> <mn>298</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> and is <math> <mn>2</mn> </math> at <math> <mrow> <mn>273</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math> The chemical process resulting in the formation of <math> <mtext>C</mtext> </math> and <math> <mtext>D</mtext> </math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** Exothermic
- **(B)** Endothermic
- **(C)** Unpredictable
- **(D)** There is no relationship between <math> <mi>ΔH</mi> </math> and <math> <mtext>K</mtext> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) Exothermic**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>log</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mn>2</mn>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mn>1</mn>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x0394;H</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>2.303</mn><mtext>R</mtext></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mfenced>   <mrow>    <mfrac>     <mn>1</mn>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>T</mtext>       <mtext>1</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfrac>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mfrac>     <mn>1</mn>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>T</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>

<math> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> <mo>&gt;</mo><msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>&amp;</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>&gt;</mo><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </math>

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>&#x0394;H</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mtext>Ve</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What will be the expression of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the given reaction if the total pressure inside the vessel is P and degree of dissociation of the reactant is a? The reaction

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mover> <mo>⇄</mo> <mrow></mrow> </mover> <mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn><msub> <mrow> <mtext>NO</mtext> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><munder><mrow><munder><mrow><mi> </mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0 </mn></mrow></munder></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m </mi></mrow></munder><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow></munder><mo>⇌</mo><munder><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><munder><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></munder></mrow></munder></math>

Total moles at equilibrium <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mtext>P</mtext><mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NO</mtext> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>a</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mtext>P</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The degree of dissociation of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>)</mo></math> obeying the equilibrium,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> , is approximately related to the pressure at equilibrium by

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/668614.PNG" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 306px; height: 101px;" />
So, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math> (if <math><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>.)

</details>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/3e33a608-23ed-4358-8921-0cc1728fd29a.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:3.69167in;height:1.25in" />
When <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are allowed to react, the equilibrium constant of the reaction at <math><mn>27</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is found <math><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>)</mo></math> .
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
What will be the equilibrium concentration of AB?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.33M
- **(B)** 2.66 M
- **(C)** 0.66 M
- **(D)** 0.33 M

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 0.66 M**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/b4d6a473-3a90-4456-b22b-652e129ffd56.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.975in;height:0.725in" />
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.66</mn></math> .

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equivalent amounts of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> are heated in a closed vessel till equilibrium is obtained. If 80% of the hydrogen can be converted to <math> <mrow>  <mtext>HI,</mtext></mrow></math>the <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math> at this temperature is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 64
- **(B)** 16
- **(C)** 0.25
- **(D)** 4

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 64**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let here be 1M each of H<sub>2</sub> and I<sub>2</sub>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math>
1                       1                  0
(1-0.8)           (1-0.8)          <math><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.8</mn></math>
=0.2                =0.2              =1.6
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mfenced close="]" open="[">       <mrow>        <mtext>HI</mtext></mrow>      </mfenced></mrow>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msup>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>I</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>&#x00A0;=</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>1.6</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>0.2</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>0.2</mn></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>64</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q12. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>mole</mtext></mrow> </math> of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> at <math> <mrow> <mtext>300</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> is kept in a closed container under <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math>. It is heated to <math> <mrow> <mtext>600</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> when <math> <mrow> <mtext>20%</mtext></mrow> </math> by mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> decomposes to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>. The resultant pressure is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.2 atm
- **(B)** 2.4 atm
- **(C)** 2.0 atm
- **(D)** 1.0 atm

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 2.4 atm**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <mtable equalrows='true' equalcolumns='true'>   <mtr>    <mtd>     <mrow></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>N</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      <msub>       <mtext>O</mtext>       <mtext>4</mtext>      </msub>      <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mo>&#x21CC;</mo>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>2NO</mtext></mrow>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>   </mtr>   <mtr>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <mtext>Initial</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>mole</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mn>1</mn>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mn>0</mn>    </mtd>   </mtr>   <mtr>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <mtext>At</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>equi</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <mtext>(1-0</mtext><mtext>.20)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>mole</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow></mrow>    </mtd>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.40</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>mole</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>   </mtr>     </mtable></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>total</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.2</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>total</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>total</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext>RT</mtext></mrow> <mtext>V</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math>
as <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Ammonium sulphide and ammonium selenide on heating dissociates as

(NH<sub>4</sub>)<sub>2</sub>S(s) <math><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow></math> 2NH<sub>3</sub>(g)+ H<sub>2</sub>S(g); K<sub>p1</sub> = 9 × 10<sup>-3</sup> atm<sup>3</sup>

(NH<sub>4</sub>)<sub>2</sub>Se(s) <math><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow></math> 2NH<sub>3</sub>(g) + H<sub>2</sub>Se(g); K<sub>p2</sub> = 4.5 × 10<sup>-3</sup> atm<sup>3</sup>

The total pressure over the solid mixture at equilibrium is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.15 atm
- **(B)** 0.3 atm
- **(C)** 0.45 atm
- **(D)** 0.6 atm

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 0.45 atm**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mtable equalcolumns="true" equalrows="true"> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow> <mn>4</mn> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>S</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>s</mtext> </mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>2NH</mtext></mrow> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>S</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mrow> <mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2p</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>p</mtext></mrow> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable></mrow> </math>

<math xmlns='http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML'> <mrow>  <mtable equalrows='true' equalcolumns='true'>   <mtr>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mfenced>         <mrow>          <msub>           <mrow>            <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>           <mn>4</mn>          </msub>          </mrow>        </mfenced></mrow>       <mn>2</mn>      </msub>      <mtext>Se</mtext><mfenced>       <mtext>s</mtext>      </mfenced><mo>&#x21CC;</mo><msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>2NH</mtext></mrow>       <mn>3</mn>      </msub>      <mfenced>       <mtext>g</mtext>      </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mn>2</mn>      </msub>      <mtext>Se</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mfenced>       <mtext>g</mtext>      </mfenced></mrow>    </mtd>   </mtr>   <mtr>    <mtd>     <mrow>      <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>2q</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x205F;</mtext><mtext>q</mtext></mrow>    </mtd>   </mtr>     </mtable></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>S</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>9.0</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>S</mtext> <mtext>e</mtext> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>q</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <mtext>q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>4.5</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>q</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mo>⇒</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>q</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>1</mn> </msub> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>2p</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>·</mo> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>9.0</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.1</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>and</mtext> <mspace width="0.5em"></mspace> <mtext>q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>0.1</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.05</mtext> </mrow> </math>

Total pressure = 2p + 2q + p + q = 3p + 3q

                        = 3 × 0.1 + 3 × 0.05 = 0.45 atm

</details>

### Q14. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the pair of reaction given below,

(i) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

(ii) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

If at a particular temperature, <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math> and <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math> are the equilibrium constants for reaction (i) and (ii) respectively then,

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>2K</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>2K</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  </mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msubsup>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>       <mtext>3</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msubsup>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>N</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    <msubsup>     <mrow>      <mtext>.p</mtext></mrow>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mtext>3</mtext>    </msubsup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math> ...(i)

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>       <mtext>3</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msubsup>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>N</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mtext>1</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    </msubsup>    <msubsup>     <mrow>      <mtext>.p</mtext></mrow>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mtext>3</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    </msubsup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

<math> <mrow>  <msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msubsup>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>       <mtext>3</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msubsup>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>N</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    <msubsup>     <mrow>      <mtext>.p</mtext></mrow>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>H</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mtext>3</mtext>    </msubsup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math> ...(ii)

Dividing equation (ii) by equation (i)

<math> <mrow>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>P</mtext>       <mtext>1</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mrow>      <msubsup>       <mtext>P</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msubsup>      </mrow>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>=1</mtext></mrow></math>

or <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q15. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Equilibrium constant for reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mn>1.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>19</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math> .
Hence equilibrium constant for ionization
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> . The value of ' <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> ' is.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

Multiply this by <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math> and divide also

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mo>-</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mn>1.8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>1.0</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q16. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate the value of equilibrium constant of the reaction at <math><mtext>22</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>7</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> , if the  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup></math>  for the reaction  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math>  is - 4.606 kcal .   <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mtext>2.0</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>.</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 100
- **(B)** 10
- **(C)** 2
- **(D)** 0.01

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 100**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>2.303</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo> </mo><mtext>log</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> 

 <math><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>4.606</mtext><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mo> </mo><mtext>2.303</mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext>0.002</mtext><mo>×</mo><mtext>500</mtext><mo> </mo><mtext>log</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> 

 <math><mtext>log</mtext><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mtext>100</mtext></math> .

</details>

### Q17. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Calculate <math> <mrow> <msub> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <mtext>G</mtext> </mrow> </math> for the reaction at <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>27</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>Given : P</mtext> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.5 bar</mtext> <mo>;</mo> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>M</mo> </mrow> </math> ;

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>M</mo> <mo>;</mo> <msub> <mo>Δ</mo> <mtext>f</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>77.1 kJ/mol</mtext> </mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mo>154.2 kJ/mol</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mo>178.9 kJ/mol</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mo>129.5 kJ/mol</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mo>129.5 kJ/mol</mo> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>77.1</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>154.2 kJ/mol</mtext></mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>Q</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> <mo>&middot;</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

   <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>0.5</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>RT ln Q</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mo>&Delta;</mo> <mtext>r</mtext> </msub> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>154.2</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>8.314</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mtext>300 ln</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

          <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo> <mo>129.5 kJ/mol</mo> </mrow> </math>
