### Q18. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the following reaction

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math>

Given: <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> , AgCl = - 112.44 kJ/mol; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo></mrow></msup></math> = - 130 kJ/mol ; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> = 75 kJ/mol
Report your answer by rounding it upto nearest whole number. The <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> of AgCl is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math> .The value of 'n' is.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **1**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>→</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> , AgCl = - 112.44 kJ/mol; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo></mrow></msup></math> = - 130 kJ/mol ; <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> = 75 kJ/mol

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup></math> , AgCl- [ <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msubsup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo></mrow></msup></math> ]

= - 112.44 kJ-[75 kJ- 130 kJ]

= -57.44 kJ

-2.303RTlog <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msubsup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>log</mi><mo>⁡</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>57440</mn><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>298</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>≅</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math>

Hence n = 1

</details>

### Q19. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the decomposition of the compound, represented as

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>COONH</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

the <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>2.9</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mo>atm</mo> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>. If the reaction is started with 1 mol of the compound, the total pressure at equilibrium would be :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow>  <mtext>7.66</mtext>  <mo>&times;</mo>  <mn>1</mn>  <msup>          <mn>0</mn>      <mrow>        <mo>-</mo>        <mn>2</mn>      </mrow>  </msup>  <mo>atm</mo></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mrow>  <mtext>38.8</mtext>  <mo>&times;</mo>  <mn>1</mn>  <msup>          <mn>0</mn>      <mrow>        <mo>-</mo>        <mn>2</mn>      </mrow>  </msup>  <mo>atm</mo></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>5.82</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>atm</mo> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mtext>1.94</mtext> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext> atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <mtext>5.82</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>atm</mo> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In case of heterogeneous equilibrium. The achive mass of solid is unit so not consider in the expression of <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>p</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>and</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>COONH</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

At equilibrium <math> <mrow> <mo>⇒</mo> </mrow> </math> 2 p    p 

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>P</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>P</mtext>       <mrow>        <msub>         <mrow>          <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>         <mtext>3</mtext>        </msub>        </mrow>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msup>  <mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>P</mtext>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mrow>        <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow>       <mtext>2</mtext>      </msub>      </mrow>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfenced><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <mtext>2P</mtext></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msup>  <mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mtext>P</mtext>  </mfenced><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>4P</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mroot> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mroot> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>Σ</mi> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mtext>Total</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>equilibrium</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mtext>P</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

### Q20. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which In <math> <mrow> <mtext>K</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>vs</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>1/T</mtext></mrow> </math> plot is correct for an equilibrium that shifts toward reactants at higher temperatures?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0003909-A.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 180px; height: 183px;" />
- **(B)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0003909-B.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 180px; height: 166px;" />
- **(C)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0003909-C.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="height: 185px; width: 180px;" />
- **(D)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0003909-d.png" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 180px; height: 172px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtext>In</mtext> <mtext>K</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>T</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>S</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

At high temperature if shifted backward, it is exothermic reaction.

<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>reac</mtext> <mtext>o</mtext> </msubsup> </mrow> </math>  is  -ve

### Q21. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mn>10</mn></math> moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> , <math><mn>15</mn></math> moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><mn>5</mn></math> moles of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are placed in a <math><mn>2</mn></math> litre vessel and allowed the come to equilibrium. The final concentration of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is <math><mn>10.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> ,

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mover> <mo>⇌</mo> <mrow></mrow> </mover> <mn>2</mn><mtext>AB</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

Determine the value of equilibrium constant <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> for the reaction.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>25.3</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>31.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>36.3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>40.5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>31.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/uztvcywnbgssazpjkylfwplaeoxpfcecltcwddkhzivnqrmqsy.jpeg" />

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>10.5</mn></math> <math> <mrow> <mo>;</mo><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow> </math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">2</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>15</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>21</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>31.5</mn></math>

</details>

### Q22. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>300</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K,</mtext> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>A</mtext><munder> <mo>⇌</mo> <mrow></mrow> </munder> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>Product</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>Δ</mi><msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>T</mtext> <mi>o</mi> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>200</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>kJmol</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>B</mtext><munder> <mo>⇌</mo> <mrow></mrow> </munder> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>Product</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mi>Δ</mi><msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>T</mtext> <mi>o</mi> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>50</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>kJmol</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </math>

Thus, the ratio of equilibrium constant at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mtext>300</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext> </math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>100</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>1000</mn></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>10000</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(D)** none of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>10000</mn></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>2.303 RT log</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </msub> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>2.303 RT log</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </msub> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>log</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </msub> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>log</mtext> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </msub> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>A</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>B</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>4</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn><mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q23. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The ionisation constant of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> in water is 5.6 <math><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math> at 25 <math><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> . The rate constant for the reaction of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> to form <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> at <math><mn>25</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is <math><mn>3.4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> . The rate constant for proton transfer from water to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>6.07</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0.607</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>60.7</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6.07</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>6.07</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math> …(i)
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>⇌</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup></math> …(ii)
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></math>
By subtracting equation (ii) from equation (i) we get
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
From equation
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3.4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>?</mo></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
So <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3.4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>5.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>6.07</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q24. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>HS</mi><mo> </mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo> </mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo> </mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
In the above reaction, if the pressure at equilibrium and at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>atm</mi></math> then what will be the equilibrium constant <math><msub><mrow><mn>K</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>p</mn></mrow></msub></math>?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2500 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>50</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>100</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2500 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wertwdfgwer.png" style="width: 450px; height: 57px;" />
Given,

Total pressure at equilibrium <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mi>atm</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mi>atm</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mo> </mo><mi>atm</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo> </mo><mo>=</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></msub></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow></msub></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>50</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2500</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi>atm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

</details>

### Q25. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

What is the equilibrium expression for the reaction
<math><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext>s</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>P</mtext>       <mn>4</mn>      </msub>      <msub>       <mtext>O</mtext>       <mrow>        <mn>10</mn></mrow>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <msub>       <mtext>P</mtext>       <mn>4</mn>      </msub>      </mrow>    </mfenced><msup>     <mrow>      <mfenced close="]" open="[">       <mrow>        <msub>         <mtext>O</mtext>         <mn>5</mn>        </msub>        </mrow>      </mfenced></mrow>     <mn>5</mn>    </msup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In the expression for equilibrium constant <math><mo>(</mo><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>p</mtext></mrow></msub><mtext> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">o</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">r</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>)</mo></math> species in solid state are not written (<math><mtext>i</mtext><mo>.</mo><mtext>e</mtext></math>., their molar concentrations are taken as 1)

<math><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>5</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext>s</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

Thus, <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>c</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q26. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The <math> <mrow> <mtext>pH</mtext></mrow> </math> of pure water at <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>308</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> are <math> <mtext>7</mtext> </math> and <math> <mtext>6</mtext> </math> respectively. Calculate the heat of formation of water from <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo></mrow></msup></math> .

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>44.981</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>44.981</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

At <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K;</mtext></mrow> </math> <math><mo>[</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>]</mo><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>w</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
At <math> <mrow> <mtext>308</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K;</mtext></mrow> </math> <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>w</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
Now using
<math> <mrow> <mn>2.303</mn><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>log</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> </msub> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>Kw</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>Kw</mtext></mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>ΔH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>R</mtext> </mfrac> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>−</mo><msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <mo>×</mo><msub> <mtext>T</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mn>2.303</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>log</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>  </msub>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mn>10</mn></mrow>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>12</mn></mrow>    </msup>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mrow>      <mn>10</mn></mrow>     <mrow>      <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow>    </msup>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>&#x0394;H</mtext></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <mfenced close="]" open="[">   <mrow>    <mfrac>     <mrow>      <mn>308</mn><mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>298</mn></mrow>     <mrow>      <mn>298</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>308</mn></mrow>    </mfrac>    </mrow>  </mfenced></mrow></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>84551.4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Thus <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>⇌</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>84.551</mn><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q27. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the atomization of Br<sub>2</sub>(g) according to the reaction :
<math><mrow><msub><mtext>Br</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>Br</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></math>       {Given antiloge(-7.02)=9x10-4}
If the heat of atomization of bromine gas is 82 KJ/mol ; standard molar entropies of Br(g) and Br<sub>2</sub>(g) are 175 and 245.4 JK<sup>-1</sup>  respectively, calculate degree of dissociation when the total pressure is 40 atm at 500 K.
(assume <math> <mi>α</mi></math>&lt;&lt;1 in your calculation)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mn>2.37</mn> </mrow> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mn>3.20</mn> </mrow> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mn>4.30</mn> </mrow> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mn>3.60</mn> </mrow> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mn>2.37</mn> </mrow> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>Br</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>Br</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>S</mtext> <mtext>reac</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>7</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>105.6</mn> <mtext> J</mtext> <msup> <mtext> K</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>reac</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mtext> KJ</mtext> <msup> <mtext> m</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mtext>reac</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>Δ</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>reac</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msubsup> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>S</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>105.6</mn> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mtext>J</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>RT </mtext> <mtext>ln </mtext> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>ln </mtext> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>7</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>7.02</mn> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><msup> <mtext>α</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mtext>P</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mtext>α</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
as <math> <mrow> <mtext>α</mtext><mo>&lt;</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mtext>α</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mtext>α</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mtext>P</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∵</mo><mtext> </mtext><mtext>P</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>40</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>9</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>⋅</mo><msup> <mi>α</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>⋅</mo><mn>40</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mtext>α</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>9</mn> <mrow> <mn>160</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mn>9</mn> <mrow> <mn>16</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mtext> </mtext><mn>2.37</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q28. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Equilibrium constant for reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>dH</mi><mo>⊕</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mfenced></math> is 1.8 <math><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></math> . Hence equilibrium constant for ionization <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> is.The value of ‘x’ is.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **18**

xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>aq</mi></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msubsup><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>aq</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>aq</mi></mfenced></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msubsup><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced></mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi></mrow></mfenced></mfrac></math></p><p>Multiply this by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></math> and divide also </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msubsup><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo></msubsup></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.8</mo><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>9</mn></msup><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.0</mo><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>18</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>value of x = 18.

### Q29. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The vapour density of N<sub>2</sub>O<sub>4</sub> at a certain temperature is 30. What is the percentage dissociation of N<sub>2</sub>O<sub>4</sub> at this temperature ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 53.3 %
- **(B)** 106.6 %
- **(C)** 26.7 %
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 53.3 %**

**Detailed Solution:**

The reaction is

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>NO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> </math>

mol. wt. of N<sub>2</sub>O<sub>4</sub><sub> </sub>= 14 x 2 + 16 x 4 = 92

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> </mrow> </math> Vapour density (D) of N<sub>2</sub>O<sub>4</sub> = 92/2 = 46

We know that,

<math> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>D</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>d</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>n</mtext> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mtext>d</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow></mfrac></mrow></math>

where,

D = Initial vapour density of reactant

d = vapour density of equilibrium mixture

<math> <mi>&#x03B1;</mi></math>= degree of dissociation

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> <mi>α</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.533</mtext> </mrow> </math>

        <math> <mrow> <mo>= </mo> <mtext>53.3 %</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q30. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> AB is 33% dissociated at a total pressure of p. Then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/648437.PNG" style="width: 350px; height: 140px;" />
<math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Σ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></math>
So <math><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> .

</details>

---

### Q31. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium constants \(K_{P 1}\) and \(K_{P 2}\) for the reaction \(X \rightleftharpoons 2 Y\) and \(Z \rightleftharpoons P+Q\), respectively are in the ratio of \(1:\) 9. If the degree of dissociation of \(X\) and \(Z\) be equal then the ratio of total pressure at these equilibria is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>36</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>9</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>1</mn><mo>:</mo><mn>36</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mtext>X</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>2Y</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </math>

                        1                    0 

At equilibrium    <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </math>              <math> <mrow> <mtext>2x</mtext></mrow> </math>

Total no. of moles = <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>2x</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>1+x</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>.p</mtext></mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>1+x</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><msub> <mrow> <mtext>.p</mtext></mrow> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>4x</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1+x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>4x</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mo>−</mo><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mtext>Z</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>P</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>)</mo></mrow><mtext>+Q</mtext><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mtext>g</mtext> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </math>

                        1                             0            0

At equilibrium  <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </math>                      <math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math>            <math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math>

Similarly, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1+x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mo>−</mo><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>p1</mtext></mrow> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>p2</mtext></mrow> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>9</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

So, <math> <mrow>  <mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>4</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mtext>1</mtext>   <mtext>9</mtext>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

<math> <mrow>  <mo>&#x21D2;</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mtext>1</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>p</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mtext>1</mtext>   <mrow>    <mtext>36</mtext></mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q32. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Hydrogen peroxide (H<sub>2</sub>O<sub>2</sub> ) decomposes according to the equation

<math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>I</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

From the following data at <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext> </mrow> </math>  calculate the value of K<sub>p</sub> at 400 K for the above reaction, <math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mtext> KJ   </mtext> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>S</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mtext>J/K</mtext> </mrow> </math>.
[Given: <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.15</mn></mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo><mn>1.41</mn></mrow> </math>]

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.14 x 10<sup>32</sup>
- **(B)** 0.14 x 10<sup>-32</sup>
- **(C)** 0.14 x 10<sup>3</sup>
- **(D)** 1.3 x 10<sup>15</sup>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 0.14 x 10<sup>32</sup>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>+</mo> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>H</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>T</mtext> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>S</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math>

         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>6</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </math>

         <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext>J</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>3</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext> log</mtext> <msub> <mtext> K</mtext> <mtext>p </mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math><mrow><mtext>log </mtext> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mn>8</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </math>

     <math> <mrow><msub><mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>Antilog</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","><mrow><mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>·</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

         <math> <mrow><mo>=</mo> <mn>0.1</mn><mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

</details>

---

### Q33. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<u><strong><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/EM0024485_Quest_1.jpg" style="width: 208px; height: 124px;" /></strong></u>

The gas A<sub>2</sub> in the left flask allowed to react with gas B<sub>2</sub> present in right flask as, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>A</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>AB</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math> ; K<sub>c</sub> = 4 at 27<sup>o</sup>C. What is the concentration of AB when equilibrium is established?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.33 M
- **(B)** 2.66 M
- **(C)** 0.66 M
- **(D)** 0.33 M

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 0.66 M**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/WhatsApp_Image_2020-07-25_at_3.05.08_PM.jpeg" style="width: 300px; height: 67px;" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced close="]" open="["><mi>AB</mi></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="["><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>c</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mtext>x</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mfenced><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒  </mo> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.33 mole</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>AB</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.33</mtext></mrow> <mn>4</mn> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.66 M</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q34. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> at <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>250</mtext></mrow> <mtext>o</mtext> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext></mrow> </math> is <math> <mrow> <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.82</mtext></mrow> </math>. Calculate the degree of dissociation at given temperature under a pressure of <math> <mrow> <mtext>5</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math>. What will be the degree of dissociation if the equilibrium pressure is <math> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>atm</mtext></mrow> </math> at same temperature.

**Options:**

- **(A)** 27.5%
- **(B)** 23%
- **(C)** 35.5%
- **(D)** 40%

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 27.5%**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/648842.PNG" style="width: 495px; height: 267px;" />

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.82</mn></math>

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.82</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5.82</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.375</mn></math>

Now new pressure is 10

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> , <math><mn>0.82</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.275</mn></math> , <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>27.5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">%</mi></math>

</details>

### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Initially 3 moles of '<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>' was taken in a <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>L</mtext></mrow> </math> container. The approx. moles of A left in the container when the following equilibrium established is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math> .The value of ' <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> ' is.
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/upsjedmhygiliheurdxrvvadnmyykcluaydllvrjlbnkwdvnfd.jpeg" />
Now,
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

In the esterification <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> an equimolar mixture of alcohol and acid taken initially yields under equilibrium, the water with mole fraction = 0.333. The equilibrium constant is.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

As alcohol and acid are equimolar so the initial concentration would also be same. Suppose the initial concentration is 'C'.
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/bxjmcoyaxukekzhqzuvgadkmasqsucjbvvyzsgeacdkeaktndl.jpeg" style="height: 100px; width: 478px;" />
<math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.33</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>
