### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following gaseous equilibria with equilibrium constants <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">n</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">d</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> respectively.

<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext></math>

The equilibrium constants are related as

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(B)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

For the reaction,

<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

Equilibrium constant, <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> ...(i)

For the reaction,

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext></math>

Equilibrium constant, <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> ...(ii)

On squaring both sides in Eq.(i), we get

<math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">S</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>[</mo><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>]</mo></mrow></mfrac></math> ...(iii)

Eq. (ii) <math><mo>×</mo></math> Eq (iii), we get

<math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

or <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></mrow></mfrac></math>

or <math><msubsup><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equilibrium constant K<sub>p</sub> for the reaction <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CaCO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mo>⇌</mo><mtext>CaO</mtext><mo>+</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> is 0.82 atm at <math> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mn>7</mn> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mtext>C.</mtext> </mrow> </math>
If 1 mole of CaCO<sub>3</sub> is placed in a closed container of 20 L and heated to this temperature, what amount of CaCO<sub>3</sub> would dissociate at equilibrium?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.2 g
- **(B)** 80 g
- **(C)** 20 g
- **(D)** 50 g

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 20 g**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CaCO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mo>⇌</mo><mtext>CaO</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.82</mn><mtext> atm</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mn>0.82</mn><mtext> atm</mtext><mo>×</mo><mn>20</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>L</mtext><mo>=</mo><msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mo>×</mo><mn>0.082</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1000</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <msub> <mtext>CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </math>
No. of moles CO<sub>2</sub> = no. of moles of CaCO<sub>3</sub> decomposed = <math><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </mfrac> </math> mole
Amount of CaCO<sub>3</sub> decomposed <math> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>5</mtext> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>100</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>20</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>g</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>800</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>, the following equilibrium is established as
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>F</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>F</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
The composition of equilibrium may be determined by measuring the rate of effusion of the mixture through a pin hole. It is found that <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>800</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo> </mo><mi>atm</mi></math> mixture effuses <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.6</mo></math> times as fast as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>SO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> effuses under the similar conditions. (At. wt. of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>19</mn></math>). What is the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub></math> (in atm) ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>0.315</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>0.685</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>0.46</mn></mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>1.49</mn></mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mn>1.49</mn></mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

According to Graham's law of effusion
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mo> </mo><mo>∝</mo><mo> </mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></msqrt></mfrac></math>

<math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>r</mtext> <mtext>mix</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>r</mtext> <msub> <mtext>SO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <msqrt> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <msub> <mtext>SO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>mix</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </msqrt> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒  </mo> <mtext>2.56</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>6</mn> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>mix</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒  </mo> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>mixture</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </math>

Let mole fraction of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> is x. Therefore, mole fraction of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></math> will be <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>0.315</mo> </mrow> </math> ;

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒  </mo> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msubsup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mtext>F</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> </mrow> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <msub> <mtext>F</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>0.685P</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>0.315P</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>≃</mo> <mtext>1.49 atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The dissociation equilibrium of a gas <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>AB</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> can be represented as,
<math> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>AB</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>AB(g)</mtext><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext></mrow> </math>
The degree of dissociation is <math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math> and is small as compared to <math> <mtext>1</mtext> </math> . The expression relating the degree of dissociation <math> <mrow> <mtext>(x)</mtext></mrow> </math> with equilibrium constant <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> and total pressure <math> <mtext>P</mtext> </math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mrow>      <mtext>(2K</mtext></mrow>     <mtext>p</mtext>    </msub>    <mtext>/P)</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mfrac>     <mtext>1</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </mfrac>    </mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>p</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>/P</mtext><mtext>&#x200B;</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>2K</mtext></mrow>   <mtext>p</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>/P</mtext><mtext>&#x200B;</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>(2K</mtext></mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mtext>/P)</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>(2K</mtext></mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mtext>/P)</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math>                                 <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mrow> <mi>AB</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mi>2AB</mi><mo>⁡</mo> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mi>B</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mi>g</mi><mo>⁡</mo> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>
At equilibrium   <math> <mrow> <mtext>2(1</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x)</mtext></mrow> </math>           <math> <mrow> <mtext>2x</mtext></mrow> </math>           <math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math>
Total moles = <math> <mrow> <mtext>(2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>x)</mtext></mrow> </math>
Partial pressures
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>AB</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mtext>=</mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>2(1</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x)</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>p,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>AB</mtext></mrow> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>2x</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mtext>p,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>B</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> <mtext>=</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mtext>x</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>2+x</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mtext>p</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> <msup> <mtext>AB</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <msub> <mtext>pB</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>(</mo> <msub> <mtext>pAB</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msup> <mo>)</mo> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mo>[</mo> <mo>(</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>)</mo> <mtext>p</mtext> <msup> <mo>]</mo> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>[</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>x</mi> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>]</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>[</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>(</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mi>x</mi> <mo>)</mo> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>x</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>p</mtext> <msup> <mo>]</mo> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </msup> <mtext>P</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><msup> <mrow> <mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow> </math> (<math> <mtext>x</mtext> </math> is small)
<math> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mo>→</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>P</mtext>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>4</mn><msup>     <mtext>x</mtext>     <mtext>3</mtext>    </msup>    <mtext>p</mtext></mrow>   <mn>8</mn>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msup>     <mtext>x</mtext>     <mtext>3</mtext>    </msup>    <mtext>p</mtext></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mroot> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mtext>kp</mtext></mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> </mfrac> </mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mroot> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>PCl</mtext></mrow> <mtext>5</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> vapour decomposes on heating according to the reaction:

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>Cl</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

The density of a sample of a partially dissociated <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>PCl</mtext></mrow> <mtext>5</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> at <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>.0</mtext></mrow> </math> atm and <math> <mrow> <mtext>500</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> was found <math> <mrow> <mtext>4</mtext><mtext>.8</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>g/L</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow> </math> Calculate the degree of dissociation and <math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> </mrow> </math>  for the reaction at <math> <mrow> <mtext>500</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>.</mtext></mrow></math>
(Given:  <math> <mrow> <mtext>R</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>0.082</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>LatmK</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math> <math> <mo>,</mo><mi> </mi> <mrow><mtext>R</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>JK</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>mol</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow><mo>,</mo></math> <math> <mrow> <mi>ln</mi><mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>2.303</mn><msub> <mrow> <mi>log</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> </msub> <mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </math> )

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **23.40**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>avg</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mi>ρ</mi> <mtext>RT</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>Cl</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>Total moles</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <mtext>avg</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>M</mtext> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>8</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mi>α</mi> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴</mo> </mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mi>α</mi> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mi>α</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mi>α</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>P</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.06</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.06</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>3.6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>RT </mtext> <mtext>ln </mtext> <mtext>K</mtext> </mrow> </math>

        <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mn>8</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>303</mn> <mtext> log </mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>6</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

        <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mn>23.4</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>kJ</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></msub></math> , the degree of dissociation of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>0.2</mn></math> at <math><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>.</mo></math> Then the <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> of <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/1234i1234gasdhfasfasdf.png" style="width: 301px; height: 172px;" />

<math> <msub>  <mtext>K</mtext>  <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac>  <mrow>   <msup>    <mrow>     <mfenced close="]" open="[">      <mrow>       <msub>        <mtext>P</mtext>        <mrow>         <msub>          <mrow>           <mtext>NO</mtext></mrow>          <mtext>2</mtext>         </msub>         </mrow>       </msub>       </mrow>     </mfenced></mrow>    <mn>2</mn>   </msup>   </mrow>  <mrow>   <msub>    <mtext>P</mtext>    <mrow>     <msub>      <mtext>N</mtext>      <mn>2</mn>     </msub>     <msub>      <mtext>O</mtext>      <mtext>4</mtext>     </msub>     </mrow>   </msub>   </mrow> </mfrac> </math>

<math> <msub> <mtext> K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>4</mtext><msup> <mi>α</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mi>α</mi> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext>p</mtext> </math>

<math> <mo> ⇒</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.2</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><msup> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mn>0.2</mn></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.16</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.96</mn></mrow> </mfrac> </math>

<math> <mtext> for</mtext><mtext>  </mtext><mn>2</mn><msub> <mtext>NO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mover> <mo>⇌</mo> <mrow></mrow> </mover> <msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mo>;</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.96</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0.16</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn> </math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At high temperature, the following reaction occurs 
<math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>  
Consider the following statements regarding reaction 
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></math>  
<math><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> depends upon the amounts of CuO and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>  
<math><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>  
<math><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo></math> <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>  
Of the statements

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1 and 2 are correct
- **(B)** 1, 2 and 3 are correct
- **(C)** 1 and 4 are correct
- **(D)** 2 and 3 are correct

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 1 and 4 are correct**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></math> is correct. 
<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>∝</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></math>  
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo></math> Number of mol gaseous (Products – Reactants) 
Here <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
So <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

---

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>°</mo><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1.7 k</mn><mtext>J</mtext><mo>]</mo></math> , the equilibrium constant for the reaction <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> at <math><mn>25</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** 24
- **(B)** 2
- **(C)** 3.6
- **(D)** 0.5

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 3.6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>&#176;</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>log</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mo>&#176;</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.7</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>2.303</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>298</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>log</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.298</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>0.298</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>0.504</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mtext>K</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>?</mo></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>0.504</mn><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3.</mn><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the equilibrium <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> , at a given temperature <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></math> of AB is dissociated then <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math> will be numerically equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **8.00**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/80bb2f49-477f-4ba0-a5f7-3e98079417c8.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:1.85833in;height:0.75833in"> 
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Σ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Σ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
So, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>

</details>

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mo>,</mo></math>  initially concentrations of a and b are equal and at equilibrium the concentration of d will be twice of that of a. What will be the equilibrium constant for the reaction?

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munder><mo>⇌</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munder><mo>+</mo><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></munder></math> 

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math> 

 <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The degree of dissociation <math><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">'</mi></math> of the reaction

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

Can be related to <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> as [Given: Total pressure at equilibrium = P)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/duyrtyergbvbv.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width: 300px; height: 50px;" />
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>–</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the following three reactions <math> <mrow> <mtext>I,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>II</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>III,</mtext></mrow> </math> equilibrium constants are given
1. <math> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>;</mo><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
2. <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>CO</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>3H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
3. <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>2</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext><mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext>4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>;</mo><mo> </mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
Which of the following relations is correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>1</mtext>  </msub>  <msqrt>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mtext>2</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msqrt>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msub>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>1</mtext>  </msub>  </mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msub>  <msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>   <mtext>3</mtext>  </msubsup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msubsup>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>1</mtext>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msubsup>  </mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As equation 3 is obtained on adding equation 1  and equation 2<math><mrow><mtext>,</mtext></mrow> </math> so <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>1</mtext> </msub> <mo>⋅</mo><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mo>.</mo></mrow> </math>

</details>

For the following equilibrium <math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math> which of the following relationship among equilibrium concentrations of <math> <mrow> <mtext>X,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>Y</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mtext>Z</mtext> </math> is not feasible?

- **(C)** [X] &gt; [Y]
- **(D)** [Y] > [Z]

<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ddmxnfydorqbbchakfnvxozzaaynaeamkgdnkmbihszoclgafd.jpeg" />

<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&gt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equilibrium constant for the reaction <math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is –
Given:
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 32
- **(B)** 16
- **(C)** 2
- **(D)** 128

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 128**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn></math> ……. (1)
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>16</mn></math> ……. (2)
Add equation (1) and (2)
<math><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>8</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>128</mn></math>

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the homogenous reaction,

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>4NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>5O</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>4NO(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>6H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O(g)</mtext></mrow> </math>

the equilibrium constant <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> has the units

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** It is dimensionless

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><msup><mrow><mo>.</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>4NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>5O</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>4NO(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>6H</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O(g)</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mtext>NO</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>O</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>6</mtext> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>4</mtext> </msup> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>5</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
Hence, units of <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>conc</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>+1</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>.

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the following gases equilibrium, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></math> is found to be equal to <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math> . This is attained when temperature is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow>  <msup>   <mtext>0</mtext>   <mtext>o</mtext>  </msup>  <mtext>C</mtext></mrow></math>
- **(B)** 273 K
- **(C)** 1 K
- **(D)** 12.19 K

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 12.19 K**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>

<math> <mrow>  <mtext>T</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mtext>P</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>K</mtext>     <mtext>C</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mfrac>   <mn>1</mn>   <mtext>R</mtext>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mn>1</mn>   <mrow>    <mn>0.0821</mn></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mo>=</mo><mn>12.18</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q53. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The ionisation constant for <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> in water is <math><mn>5.0</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math> at <math><msup><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> .The rate constant for the reaction of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> to form <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math> at <math><msup><mrow><mn>25</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is <math> <mrow>  <mn>3.0</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>  </msup>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mtext>Lmol</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </msup>  <msup>   <mtext>s</mtext>   <mrow>    <mo>&#x2212;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>. The rate constant for proton transfer from water to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>. The value of 'x' is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup></math>
Now,
<math><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wdezozmsqnslpwecvkvwwujfnwboxjbplgukfsgqkmadowyicc.jpeg" />
now,
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>k</mtext>     <mtext>f</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>k</mtext>     <mtext>b</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mn>5</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mn>4</mn>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mn>3</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><msup>     <mrow>      <mn>10</mn></mrow>     <mrow>      <mn>10</mn></mrow>    </msup>    </mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>k</mtext>     <mtext>b</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>k</mtext>   <mtext>b</mtext>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mn>10</mn></mrow>   <mn>5</mn>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q54. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Which is correct relationship between <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math> for following reversible reaction at <math> <mrow> <mtext>10</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> temperature? <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math> in units of atm and <math> <mtext>M</mtext> </math> respectively
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>A</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>3B</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mtext> </mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mrow> <mtext>2AB</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mtext> &gt; </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mtext> &lt; </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mtext> = </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** Relation cannot be predicted

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mtext> &gt; </mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>p</mtext>  </msub>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced>     <mrow>      <mtext>RT</mtext></mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mrow>      <mtext>&#x0394;n</mtext></mrow>     <mtext>g</mtext>    </msub>    </mrow>  </msup>  </mrow></math>
Here,<math> <mrow> <mtext>RT</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mrow> <mtext>12</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>10</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>(&lt;1)</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>Δn</mtext></mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>2</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>4</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

### Q55. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The degree of dissociation is 0.4 at 420 K and 1.0 atm for the gaseous reaction

<math><mi>P</mi><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⟶</mo><mi>P</mi><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> . Assuming ideal behavior of all gases, calculate the density of equilibrium mixture of equilibrium mixture at 420 K and 1.0 atm. (P = 31, Cl = 35.5)

**Options:**

- **(A)** 4.30
- **(B)** 3.54
- **(C)** 2.64
- **(D)** 1.56

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 4.30**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>Cl</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>t</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>a</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>t</mtext> <mtext>eq</mtext> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>0.6a</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>0.4a</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>0.4a</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>



<math><msub><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>Total</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1.4</mn><mi>a</mi></math>

<math> <mtable columnalign='left'>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mo>&#x2234;</mo><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><msub>     <mi>M</mi>     <mrow>      <mi>a</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow>    </msub>    <mo>=</mo><mfrac>     <mrow>      <mn>208</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mi>a</mi></mrow>     <mrow>      <mn>1.4</mn><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mi>a</mi></mrow>    </mfrac>       </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mrow></mrow>   </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>148.57</mn>   </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mrow></mrow>   </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mo>&#x2234;</mo><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><msub>     <mi>P</mi>     <mrow>      <mi>m</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow>    </msub>    <mo>=</mo><mfrac>     <mrow>      <msub>       <mi>P</mi>       <mrow>        <msub>         <mi>M</mi>         <mrow>          <mi>a</mi><mi>v</mi></mrow>        </msub>        </mrow>      </msub>      </mrow>     <mrow>      <mi>R</mi><mi>T</mi></mrow>    </mfrac>       </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mrow></mrow>   </mtd>  </mtr>  <mtr>   <mtd>    <mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mtext>&#x2009;</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac>     <mrow>      <mn>1</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>148.57</mn></mrow>     <mrow>      <mn>0.0821</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>420</mn></mrow>    </mfrac>    <mo>=</mo><mn>4.30</mn>   </mtd>  </mtr> </mtable> </math>

</details>

### Q56. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction, <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>A</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>B</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <mtext>C</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>D</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <msub> <mo>; K</mo> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>. If the initial moles of <math> <mrow> <mtext>A,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>B,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>C</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mtext>D</mtext> </math> are <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow> </math> and <math> <mn>3</mn> </math> moles respectively in <math> <mrow> <mtext>1</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>L</mtext></mrow> </math> vessel, what is the equilibrium concentration of <math> <mrow> <mtext>A?</mtext></mrow> </math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <msup> <mn>10</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> <mo>&times;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>A</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>B</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>C</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>D</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>Initial moles</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>7</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>3</mn> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>At equilibrium</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>7</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> </mrow> </math>Due to very high value of <math> <mrow>  <mtext>K,</mtext></mrow></math> we can assume that reactant almost gets converted into products, so

<math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>y</mtext> <mo>⇒</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>∼</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>C</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>D</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>B</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>8</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>y</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>⇒  </mo> <mtext>y</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo> </mrow> </math>Equilibrium concentration of <math> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>y</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>4</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
