### Q57. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> . The value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math> is 4. If 2 mol of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> , 2 mol of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> and 2 mol of HI are present in one litre container then moles of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> present at equilibrium is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.8
- **(B)** 3.2
- **(C)** 2.4
- **(D)** 4.4

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 2.4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/631237.JPG" style="width: 347px; height: 125px;" />
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.4</mn></math>
Mol of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> at equilibrium <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2.4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q58. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

500 mL of 0.150 M AgNO<sub>3</sub> solution were added in 500 mL of 1.09 M Fe<sup>2</sup><sup>+</sup> solution and the reaction is allowed to reach an equilibrium at <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext></mrow></math>.

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>⇌</mo> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>aq</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

For 25 mL of the solution, 30 mL of 0.0832 M KMnO<sub>4</sub> were required for oxidation. Calculate equilibrium constant (M<sup>-1</sup>) of the reaction at <math> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <msup> <mn>5</mn> <mo>∘</mo> </msup> <mtext>C</mtext></mrow></math>.

m.eqs of <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>KMnO</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mn>3</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>5</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>8</mn> <mtext> m.eqs</mtext> </mrow> </math> 
(Z factor =5)

m.eqs of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn><mn>·</mn><mn>4</mn><mn>8</mn> <mtext>Meqs</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn><mn>·</mn><mn>4</mn><mn>8</mn> <mtext>mM</mtext> </mrow>  
(Z factor= 1)

Total mM of <math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math> left <math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>4</mn><mn>8</mn> <mo>×</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mtext> mM</mtext> </mrow> </math>

At Equilibrium concentrations

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Ag</mtext> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mtext> mM</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext> mL</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mtext> M</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mtext> mM</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext> mL</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mn>9</mn> <mtext> M</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="[" separators=","> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>Fe</mtext> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>+</mo> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mtext> mM</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mtext> mL</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mn>·</mn> <mn>0</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mn>6</mn> <mtext> M</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Ag</mtext></mrow> <mo>+</mo> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Fe</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>046</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>0</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>499</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>029</mn></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>3.18</mn><msup> <mtext>M</mtext> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

### Q59. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
The equilibrium pressure at 25°C is 0.660 atm. What is <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the reaction?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.109 <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** 0.218 <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** 1.89 <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** 2.18 <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) 0.109 <math><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>   <mn>4</mn>  </msub>  <mtext>HS</mtext><mfenced>   <mtext>s</mtext>  </mfenced><mo>&#x21CC;</mo><munder>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mrow>      <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow>     <mn>3</mn>    </msub>    <mfenced>     <mtext>g</mtext>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mtext>P</mtext>  </munder>  <mo>+</mo><munder>   <mrow>    <msub>     <mtext>H</mtext>     <mn>2</mn>    </msub>    <mtext>S</mtext><mfenced>     <mtext>g</mtext>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mtext>P</mtext>  </munder>  </mrow></math>
Equilibrium pressure is 2P here
<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.660</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.330</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>.</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.33</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.33</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0.109</mn><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q60. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium constant for the reaction
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
is 0.44 at 1260 K. The equilibrium constant for the reaction
<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
at 1260 K is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.44
- **(B)** 0.88
- **(C)** 5.16
- **(D)** 126

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 5.16**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is obtained by reversing the reaction
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> and then multiplying it by the factor '2'. Hence the equilibrium constant <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mtext>K'</mtext></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow> </math> for this reaction is given by
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>K'=</mtext><msup>   <mrow>    <mrow><mo>(</mo>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mtext>1</mtext>       <mtext>K</mtext>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow>   <mtext>2</mtext>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mrow><mo>(</mo>     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mn>1</mn>       <mrow>        <mn>0.44</mn></mrow>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  <mo>=</mo><mn>5.16</mn></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q61. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

At <math><mn>273 </mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and one atm, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">‘</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">’</mi></math> litre of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> decomposes to <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> as : <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math> To what extent has the decomposition proceeded when he original volume is 25% less then hat of existing volume?
[Report your answer up to two decimal places.]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **33.33**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>∵</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>∝</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo> </mo><mtext>a consant P and T</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mtext>I</mtext><mtext>n</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>t</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>l </mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>M</mtext><mtext>o</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mtext>M</mtext><mtext>o</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>s </mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>t </mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>e</mtext><mtext>q</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>l</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>b</mtext><mtext>r</mtext><mtext>i</mtext><mtext>u</mtext><mtext>m </mtext></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></munder><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2</mtext></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>4</mtext></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mtext></mtext><mtext></mtext><mtext>a</mtext></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mtext>(</mtext><mtext>a</mtext><mtext>-</mtext><mtext>x</mtext><mtext>)</mtext></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></munder><mo>⇌</mo><munder><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>2NO</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>2</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mtable><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mtext></mtext><mtext>0</mtext></mrow></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mrow><maligngroup></maligngroup><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mtd></mtr></mtable></mrow></munder></math>
Given original volume <math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> of existing volume at equilibrium
or initial moles<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mfenced> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>75</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>100</mtext></mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfenced><mtext>×moles at equilibrium</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>75</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
or <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>25</mn><mn>75</mn></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>·</mo><mn>33.33</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mfrac></math>
<math> <mrow> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.3333</mn><mtext>a</mtext></mrow> <mtext>a</mtext> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>0.3333</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>or</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mn>33.33</mn><mtext>%</mtext></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q62. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> , if initially the concentration of <math> <mtext>A</mtext> </math> and <math> <mtext>B</mtext> </math> are equal but at equilibrium concentration of <math> <mtext>D</mtext> </math> will be twice of that of <math> <mtext>A</mtext> </math> then what will be the equilibrium constant of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> reaction ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/633161_a.JPG" />
<math> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext><mo>−</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>
<math><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>x</mtext><mo>⋅</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext><mo>–</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced><mfenced> <mrow> <mtext>a</mtext><mo>–</mo><mtext>x</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><msup> <mtext>a</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>9</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mtext>a</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> <mo>×</mo><mfrac> <mtext>a</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>C</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>D</mtext><mo>&#x21CC;</mo><mtext>A</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>B;</mtext></mrow></math><math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mtext>K</mtext>   <mtext>c</mtext>  </msub>  <mo>=</mo><mfrac>   <mn>1</mn>   <mn>4</mn>  </mfrac>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q63. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium <math> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow> <mtext>0</mtext> </msup> <mo>+</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>Cu</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mtext>II</mtext></mrow> </msup> <mo>⇌</mo><msup> <mrow> <mtext>2Cu</mtext></mrow> <mtext>I</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>

In aqueous medium at 25oC shifts towards the left in the presence of

(1) <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> (2) <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> (3) <math><mtext>SC</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> (4) <math><mtext>N</mtext><msup><mrow><msub><mrow><mtext>O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1,2
- **(B)** 2,3
- **(C)** 1,2,3
- **(D)** 2,3,4

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) 1,2,3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>u</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  ions will react with  <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mtext>SC</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  forming  <math><mo>[</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><mo>(</mo><mtext>CN</mtext><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  and  <math><mo>[</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><mo>(</mo><mtext>SCN</mtext><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>  leading the reaction in the backward direction.

 <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>u</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>2C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> 

 <math><mtext>2Cu</mtext><mo>(</mo><mtext>CN</mtext><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>→</mo><mtext>2CuCN</mtext><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> 

 <math><mtext>CuCN</mtext><mo>+</mo><mtext>3C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mo>[</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>CN</mtext><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>u</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mtext>4SC</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>N</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mo>→</mo><mo>[</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>SCN</mtext><msub><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> 

 <math><mtext>C</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>u</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math>  also combines with  <math><mtext>CuC</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>  which reacts with Cu to produce CuCl pushing the reaction in the backward direction.

 <math><mtext>CuC</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>l</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mtext>Cu</mtext><mo>→</mo><mtext>2CuCl</mtext><mo>↓</mo></math>

</details>

### Q64. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Initially, 0.8 mol of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></math> and 0.2 mol of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> are mixed in a one litre vessel. At equilibrium, 0.4 mol of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is present. The value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the reaction, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> would be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0.13</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0.66</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>0.013</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0.05</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0.13</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/solutddddfasdfs.jpg" style="width: 450px; height: 93px;" />

So <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.2</mn></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>0.2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>0.13</mn><mtext> mol </mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>L</mtext></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q65. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

For the equilibrium <math><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>.</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>;</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> at 310 K. A 5 L vessel contains 0.1 mole of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> . How many mole of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> should be added to the flask at this temperature derive the backward reaction for completion? (use R = 0.082 atm-L/mol K)
[Report your answer upto two decimal places.]

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.79**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/ef6997aa-1ca1-49bb-b21e-d83af662807c.jpeg" style="max-width:100%; object-fit:contain" style="width:2.51667in;height:0.55in" />
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn></math>
PV = nRT
<math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mo>)</mo><mn>0.082</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>310</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.79</mn></math>

</details>

### Q66. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium constants at 298 K for a reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> is 100. If the initial concentration of all the four species were 1 M each, then equilibrium concentration of D (in mol <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> ) will be

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.182
- **(B)** 0.182
- **(C)** 0.818
- **(D)** 1.818

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 1.818**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

Hence <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> so reaction moves in forward reaction

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/wbgzoqphlvymvsgdwefxgdwufrzvdswqwojachmsqneqdkptep.jpeg" />

<math><mfrac><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

<math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

So, <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1.818</mn></math> M

</details>

### Q67. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Ammonium carbonate decomposes as

<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mtext mathvariant="normal">C</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">C</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

For the reaction, <math><msub><mrow><mtext>K</mtext></mrow><mrow><mtext>p</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2.9</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">a</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">t</mtext><msup><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">m</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>. If we start with 1 mole of the compound, the total pressure at equilibrium would be

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.766 atm
- **(B)** 0.0582 atm
- **(C)** 0.0388 atm
- **(D)** 0.0194 atm

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 0.0582 atm**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mtext mathvariant="normal">C</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>s</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">C</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mtext mathvariant="normal">g</mtext><mo>)</mo></math>

At equilibrium if partial pressure of <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">C</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">O</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"> </mtext><mtext mathvariant="normal"></mtext><mtext>is </mtext> <mtext>p</mtext></math> then that of <math><mtext mathvariant="normal">N</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext mathvariant="normal">H</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math><mn>2</mn><mtext>p</mtext></math>

So, <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msubsup> <mtext>p</mtext> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mn>2</mn> </msubsup> <mo>×</mo> <msub> <mtext>p</mtext> <msub> <mtext mathvariant="italic">CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>p</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> <mo>×</mo> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>2.9</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mtext>p</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>p</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>1.935</mtext> <mo>×</mo> <msup> <mtext>10</mtext> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

hence, total pressure <math><mtext>p</mtext><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mtext>p</mtext></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>5.81</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0.0581</mn></math> atm.

</details>

### Q68. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

20% of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> molecules are dissociated in a sample of gas at <math><mn>27</mn><mo>°</mo></math> C and 760 torr. Mixture has the density at equilibrium equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** 1.48 g/L
- **(B)** 1.84 g/L
- **(C)** 2.25 g/L
- **(D)** 3.12 g/L

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 3.12 g/L**

**Detailed Solution:**

The reaction is
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
n = 2 and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>20</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.2</mn></math>
D = 4.6, initial vapour density
d = vapour density at equilibrium
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mn>0.2</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>46</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
d = 38.3
Molar mass equilibrium <math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>38.3</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>76.6</mn></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>PM</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>dRT</mtext></mrow></math>
Here, d = density of gas mixture
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>d(mix)</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>PM</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>RT</mtext></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>=&#x00A0;</mtext><mfrac>   <mrow>    <mtext>1</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>76</mtext><mtext>.6</mtext></mrow>   <mrow>    <mtext>0</mtext><mtext>.0821</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>&#x00D7;</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>300</mtext></mrow>  </mfrac>  <mtext>&#x2004;</mtext></mrow></math>
<math> <mrow>  <mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>3</mtext><mtext>.12</mtext><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>g/L</mtext></mrow></math>

</details>

### Q69. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider the following reactions in which all the reactants and the products are in gaseous state.
<math><mtext>2PQ</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mtext>PQ</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext><mo>;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
The value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math> for the equilibrium
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>Q</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>1.0</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mn>1.0</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given , <math><mtext>2PQ</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><msup><mrow><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>3.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mtext>PQ</mtext><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext><mo>;</mo></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>PQ</mtext></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math>
For the required equilibrium
<math><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>P</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>Q</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mtext>2</mtext><msub><mrow><mtext>R</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>⇌</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext><mo>;</mo></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mtext>PQR</mtext><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>×</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>3.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>5</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>0.6</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>3</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q70. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

At a definite temperature, the equilibrium constant for a reaction,  <math> <mrow> <mtext>A</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>B</mtext> <mo>⇌</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>C</mtext> </mrow> </math> , was found to be 81. Starting with 1 mole A and 1 mole B, the mole fraction of C at equilibrium is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow>  <mfrac>    <mrow>      <mn>2</mn>    </mrow>    <mrow>      <mn>1</mn>      <mn>1</mn>    </mrow>  </mfrac></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow>  <mfrac>    <mrow>      <mn>7</mn>    </mrow>    <mrow>      <mn>1</mn>      <mn>1</mn>    </mrow>  </mfrac></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>A</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>B</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>C</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴     </mo> <mn>8</mn> <mn>1</mn> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mtext>x</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

or     <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>x</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴     </mo> <mtext>x</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

Thus,   <math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <mtext>A</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mtext>B</mtext> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>C</mtext> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn></mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn></mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>18</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>(</mo></math> Total mole <math> <mtext></mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn></mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn></mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>+</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>18</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn></mn> <mn>22</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>)</mo> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>∴  </mo></math> Mole fraction of  <math> <mo>C=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>18</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>9</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q71. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<p>The degree of dissociation of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> for the equilibrium <math><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> is approximately related to the pressure at equilibrium (P) by the relation <math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>≪</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>]</mo></math></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>∝</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mtable> <mtr> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>5</mn> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>⇌</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>PCl</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mo>+</mo> </mtd> <mtd> <msub> <mtext>Cl</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> </mtd> <mtd> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>Total pressure</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>P</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mn>0</mn> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> </mtr> <mtr> <mtd> <mn>1</mn> <mo>-</mo> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> <mtd> <mi>α</mi> </mtd> <mtd> </mtd> </mtr> </mtable> </mrow> </math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi> </mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>≈</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>≪</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

Neglecting '<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>'

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math>

<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">α</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math><p> </p>

</details>

### Q72. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For the reaction, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> the degree of dissociation at equilibrium is 0.4 at a pressure of 1 atm. ### Q73. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium constant of the following isomerisation reaction at <math> <mrow> <mtext>400</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> are <math> <mrow> <mtext>2</mtext><mtext>.07</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mtext>3</mtext><mtext>.42</mtext></mrow> </math> respectively.
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/pxykewhronxzhthpfahykuzychjoixgbfkfvlmkaqkazmcipge.jpeg" style="height: 45px; width: 250px;" />
Which of the following is/are correct?
I. The reaction is exothermic
II. The reaction is endothermic
III. At <math> <mrow> <mtext>400</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> <math> <mrow> <mtext>50%</mtext></mrow> </math> of cis-butene and <math> <mrow> <mtext>50%</mtext></mrow> </math> of trans-butene are present of equilibrium
IV. Both at <math> <mrow> <mtext>298</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math> and <math> <mrow> <mn>400</mn><mtext> </mtext><mtext>K</mtext></mrow> </math>, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** I and IV
- **(B)** II and IV
- **(C)** I and III
- **(D)** I only

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) I only**

**Detailed Solution:**

As on increasing temperature  <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">q</mi></mrow></msub></math>  is increasing so  reaction is exothermic.

</details>

### Q74. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Equilibrium constant <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the following reaction at 800 K is, 4
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
The value of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the following reaction will be
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>800</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>16</mn><mo>×</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>800</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>800</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>800</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>800</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mo>⇌</mo><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>N</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g)</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>+</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>3</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>H</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> <mtext>(g);</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext> </mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>4</mtext></mrow> </math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
So <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the following reaction will be equal to <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></msup></math>
<math> <mrow> <mtext>=</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mfrac> <mtext>1</mtext> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>4</mtext> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> <mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>(800</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>×</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mtext>R)</mtext></mrow> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mtext>2</mtext></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>
<math> <mrow>  <mo>=</mo><msup>   <mrow>    <mfenced close="]" open="[">     <mrow>      <mfrac>       <mn>1</mn>       <mrow>        <mn>4</mn><mo>&#x00D7;</mo><mn>800</mn><mtext>&#x2004;</mtext><mtext>R</mtext></mrow>      </mfrac>      </mrow>    </mfenced></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </msup>  </mrow></math>

</details>

### Q75. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The equilibrium, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></math> , is followed to set-up at <math><mn>127</mn><mo>°</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> in a closed vessel. The total pressure at equilibrium was 20 atm. The <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math> for the reaction is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0.092 </mn><mtext></mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>M</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0.085 </mn><mtext></mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>M</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3.045 </mn><mtext></mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>M</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** None of these

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0.092 </mn><mtext></mtext><msup><mrow><mtext>M</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>20</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>atm</mtext></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>×</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo> </mo><mtext>at</mtext><msup><mtext>m</mtext><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Δ</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mn>100</mn><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>(</mo><mn>0.0821</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>400</mn><msup><mrow><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.092</mn><mo> </mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math> .

</details>
